# Exploration: 10000 randomized runs through two jumps

Each run draws a random player (persona) and random choices each turn, with its own seed; the simulation seed also differs per run. Generated by `dotnet run --project src/Butterfly.Batch -- --explore 10000`.

## Bugs and anomalies
None found.
- Runs holding 10%+ of both Senate factions at departure (the rule says holding 10% of one shuts you out of the other): 0.
- Economic busts while away: 779 runs, 1514 busts (up to 2 in one run); their mean second-arrival Index 102.1 vs 118.9 without.
- Wrongness beat describing the debased coin twice ("copper washed in silver" and "bronze washed thin"): 0 first arrivals.
- Determinism: 10 runs replayed with identical log hashes.
- Refused commands: 1061555 of 2801605 (random players try things they can't do; not bugs by themselves).

## Do big choices lead to different places?

## Founded institutions
- Founded: 256; failed before departure: 31 (12%).
- Failed ones: median years it lasted 1; strength when it failed: median 19.0; took rival strikes: 23% (median 0); the player invested in it: 32%.
- Survivors: strength at departure median 40.3; invested in: 79%; took rival strikes: 66%.
